FILE: config.py

from pathlib import Path
# Questo trova automaticamente la cartella dove si trova QUESTO file (la root)
ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.resolve()

MODEL_PATH = ROOT_DIR / "models"

DATASET_BASIC_PATH = ROOT_DIR / "dataset"
DATASET_TR23 = DATASET_BASIC_PATH / "tracking-2023"
DATASET_TR23_TEST = DATASET_TR23 / "test"
DATASET_TR23_TRAIN = DATASET_TR23 / "train"


YOLO_DATASET_PATH = DATASET_TR23 / "dataset_yolo"
SPLIT_DIR = YOLO_DATASET_PATH / "splits"

OUTPUT_DIR = ROOT_DIR/ "out"
DETECTOR_OUTPUT_DIR = OUTPUT_DIR / "detector_results"
TRACKER_OUTPUT_DIR = OUTPUT_DIR / "tracker_results"

SEQMAP_DIR = OUTPUT_DIR / "seqmap"
SEQMAP_FILE = SEQMAP_DIR / "soccer_test.txt"

TRACKER_CONFIG = ROOT_DIR / "src" / "Tracker" / "Yolo12m"

FILE: config.py

from pathlib import Path
# Questo trova automaticamente la cartella dove si trova QUESTO file (la root)
ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.resolve()

MODEL_PATH = ROOT_DIR / "models"

DATASET_BASIC_PATH = ROOT_DIR / "dataset"
DATASET_TR23 = DATASET_BASIC_PATH / "tracking-2023"
DATASET_TR23_TEST = DATASET_TR23 / "test"
DATASET_TR23_TRAIN = DATASET_TR23 / "train"


YOLO_DATASET_PATH = DATASET_TR23 / "dataset_yolo"
SPLIT_DIR = YOLO_DATASET_PATH / "splits"

OUTPUT_DIR = ROOT_DIR/ "out"
DETECTOR_OUTPUT_DIR = OUTPUT_DIR / "detector_results"
TRACKER_OUTPUT_DIR = OUTPUT_DIR / "tracker_results"

SEQMAP_DIR = OUTPUT_DIR / "seqmap"
SEQMAP_FILE = SEQMAP_DIR / "soccer_test.txt"

TRACKER_CONFIG = ROOT_DIR / "src" / "Tracker" / "Yolo12m" 


================================================
FILE: conda/build_run.bat
================================================

@echo off
echo [INFO] Avvio procedura combinata Build & Run...

:: Chiama lo script di build
call .\conda\build\build.bat

:: Controlla se c'ÃƒÂ¨ stato un errore nella build
if %errorlevel% neq 0 (
    echo [ERROR] La creazione dell'environment ha fallito. Impossibile eseguire il run.
    pause
    exit /b %errorlevel%
)

echo [INFO] Build completata con successo. Avvio applicazione...
echo ---------------------------------------------------------

:: Chiama lo script di run
call conda\run\run.bat


================================================
FILE: conda/build_run.sh
================================================

#!/bin/bash

echo "[INFO] Avvio procedura combinata Build & Run..."

# Assicura che gli script interni abbiano i permessi di esecuzione
chmod +x conda/build/build.sh
chmod +x conda/run/run.sh

# Esegue la build. 
# L'operatore '&&' assicura che il 'run' parta solo se la 'build' finisce senza errori.
./conda/build/build.sh && \
echo "[INFO] Build completata. Avvio applicazione..." && \
echo "---------------------------------------------------------" && \
./conda/run/run.sh


================================================
FILE: conda/delete/delete.bat
================================================
@echo off
echo Removing Conda environment 'soccer_tracking'...
call conda env remove -n soccer_tracking
echo Deletion complete.
pause


================================================
FILE: conda/delete/delete.sh
================================================
#!/bin/bash
echo "Removing Conda environment 'soccer_tracking'..."
conda env remove -n soccer_tracking
echo "Deletion complete."


================================================
FILE: conda/run/run.bat
================================================
@echo off
call conda activate soccer_tracking
python main.py
pause


================================================
FILE: conda/run/run.sh
================================================
#!/bin/bash
# Initialize conda for shell interaction
eval "$(conda shell.bash hook)"
conda activate soccer_tracking

# Run the main script
# Adjust the path to your python script if necessary
python main.py

================================================
FILE: src/Dataset/clean_gt.py
================================================
import os
import configparser
import shutil
from config import *

def clean_gt_for_soccer():
    # Percorso ai tuoi dati di test (o train se stai facendo prove)
    base_paths = [DATASET_TR23_TEST, DATASET_TR23_TRAIN]
    
    # Classi che consideriamo "TARGET" da tracciare
    # In base al PDF, il task ÃƒÂ¨ "Player tracking". 
    # Solitamente include portieri. Gli arbitri a volte sono esclusi o inclusi.
    # Se il gameinfo distingue "referee", decidi tu se tenerlo. 
    # Qui sotto tengo players, goalkeepers e referee.
    valid_keywords = ['player', 'goalkeeper', 'referee'] 

    
    for base_path in base_paths:
        if not os.path.exists(base_path):
            print(f"Errore: {base_path} non trovato.")
            return
        
        for seq_name in os.listdir(base_path):
            seq_path = os.path.join(base_path, seq_name)
            gameinfo_path = os.path.join(seq_path, 'gameinfo.ini')
            gt_folder = os.path.join(seq_path, 'gt')
            gt_path = os.path.join(gt_folder, 'gt.txt')
            
            # Facciamo un backup del gt originale se non esiste
            gt_backup = os.path.join(gt_folder, 'gt_backup.txt')

            if os.path.exists(gameinfo_path) and os.path.exists(gt_path):
                if not os.path.exists(gt_backup):
                    shutil.copy(gt_path, gt_backup)
                
                # Leggiamo gli ID da rimuovere (es. la palla)
                config = configparser.ConfigParser()
                config.read(gameinfo_path)
                
                ids_to_keep = []
                if 'Sequence' in config:
                    for key, value in config['Sequence'].items():
                        if key.startswith('trackletid_'):
                            # Se la descrizione contiene player/goalkeeper/referee, teniamo l'ID
                            if any(k in value.lower() for k in valid_keywords):
                                try:
                                    # trackletID_20 -> prendiamo 20
                                    obj_id = int(key.split('_')[1])
                                    ids_to_keep.append(obj_id)
                                except:
                                    pass

                # Riscriviamo il GT pulito
                with open(gt_backup, 'r') as f_in, open(gt_path, 'w') as f_out:
                    for line in f_in:
                        parts = line.strip().split(',')
                        # Formato GT: frame, ID, x, y, w, h, conf, class, vis
                        if len(parts) < 6: continue
                        
                        obj_id = int(parts[1])
                        
                        if obj_id in ids_to_keep:
                            # TRUCCO TECNICO:
                            # Assicuriamoci che ci siano 10 colonne (standard MOT)
                            # E impostiamo la colonna 8 (classe) a 1 ("pedestrian" per TrackEval)
                            # Anche se sono calciatori, TrackEval vuole vedere "1" lÃƒÂ¬.
                            
                            # Recuperiamo i dati essenziali
                            frame = parts[0]
                            pid = parts[1]
                            x, y, w, h = parts[2:6]
                            
                            # Creiamo la riga perfetta per TrackEval:
                            # conf=1, class=1, visibility=1
                            new_line = f"{frame},{pid},{x},{y},{w},{h},1,1,1\n"
                            f_out.write(new_line)
                
                print(f"Pulito GT per sequenza: {seq_name}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    clean_gt_for_soccer()


================================================
FILE: src/Dataset/convert_to_yolo.py
================================================
# =================================================
# FILE: src/Dataset/convert_to_yolo.py
# =================================================
import os
import shutil
import sys
import cv2
from pathlib import Path
from tqdm import tqdm

# Aggiungiamo la root al path per importare config
ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.resolve()
sys.path.append(str(ROOT_DIR))


# Definiamo il tasso di campionamento (1 frame ogni 25)
TRAIN_SUBSAMPLE_RATE = 10

# Importiamo le costanti definite in config
from config import DATASET_TR23_TRAIN, YOLO_DATASET_PATH, SPLIT_DIR

def convert_dataset_from_manifest():
    print("--- Inizio Conversione Dataset (Manifest Mode) ---")
    
    # 1. Verifica esistenza file manifest
    train_manifest = SPLIT_DIR / "train.txt"
    val_manifest = SPLIT_DIR / "val.txt"
    
    if not train_manifest.exists() or not val_manifest.exists():
        print(f"[ERRORE] File manifest non trovati in {SPLIT_DIR}")
        print("Esegui prima 'src/Dataset/create_splits.py' per generare le liste!")
        return

    # 2. Leggi le liste delle sequenze
    with open(train_manifest, 'r') as f:
        train_seqs = [line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()]
        
    with open(val_manifest, 'r') as f:
        val_seqs = [line.strip() for line in f if line.strip()]
        
    print(f"Sequenze caricate -> Train: {len(train_seqs)}, Val: {len(val_seqs)}")

    # 3. Pulisci e Prepara le cartelle di destinazione (train/val)
    # Nota: Non cancelliamo SPLIT_DIR perchÃƒÂ© ÃƒÂ¨ dentro YOLO_DATASET_PATH
    images_dir = YOLO_DATASET_PATH / "images"
    labels_dir = YOLO_DATASET_PATH / "labels"
    
    if images_dir.exists(): shutil.rmtree(images_dir)
    if labels_dir.exists(): shutil.rmtree(labels_dir)
    
    for split in ['train', 'val']:
        (images_dir / split).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        (labels_dir / split).mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    # Funzione interna per processare una lista di sequenze
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------
    def process_split(seq_list, split_name):
        dest_img_dir = images_dir / split_name
        dest_lbl_dir = labels_dir / split_name
        
        print(f"Elaborazione split: {split_name}...")
        
        for seq_name in tqdm(seq_list):
            seq_path = DATASET_TR23_TRAIN / seq_name
            gt_path = seq_path / "gt" / "gt.txt"
            img_source_dir = seq_path / "img1"
            
            # Controllo integritÃƒÂ 
            if not seq_path.exists():
                print(f"[WARN] La sequenza '{seq_name}' ÃƒÂ¨ nel manifest ma non sul disco. Saltata.")
                continue
            if not gt_path.exists():
                # Alcune sequenze potrebbero non avere GT (raro nel train set), saltiamo
                continue

            # A. Otteniamo dimensioni immagine (necessario per normalizzare YOLO)
            # Leggiamo la prima immagine disponibile
            first_img = next(img_source_dir.glob("*.jpg"), None)
            if first_img is None:
                continue
                
            img_cv = cv2.imread(str(first_img))
            if img_cv is None: continue
            img_h, img_w, _ = img_cv.shape

            # B. Leggiamo e Parsiamo il GT (Ground Truth)
            # Formato MOT: frame, id, x_top, y_top, w, h, ...
            frames_data = {}
            with open(gt_path, 'r') as f:
                for line in f:
                    parts = line.strip().split(',')
                    if len(parts) < 6: continue
                    
                    frame_id = int(parts[0])
                    x_top, y_top, w_box, h_box = float(parts[2]), float(parts[3]), float(parts[4]), float(parts[5])
                    
                    # C. Convertiamo in formato YOLO (Normalizzato 0-1 e Centro)
                    # Class ID 0 = player
                    x_center = (x_top + w_box / 2) / img_w
                    y_center = (y_top + h_box / 2) / img_h
                    w_norm = w_box / img_w
                    h_norm = h_box / img_h
                    
                    # Clipping per sicurezza (valori devono stare tra 0 e 1)
                    x_center = max(0, min(1, x_center))
                    y_center = max(0, min(1, y_center))
                    w_norm = max(0, min(1, w_norm))
                    h_norm = max(0, min(1, h_norm))
                    
                    if frame_id not in frames_data:
                        frames_data[frame_id] = []
                    
                    # Stringa YOLO: class x_c y_c w h
                    frames_data[frame_id].append(f"0 {x_center:.6f} {y_center:.6f} {w_norm:.6f} {h_norm:.6f}")

            # D. Copiamo le immagini e creiamo i file label
            for img_file in img_source_dir.glob("*.jpg"):
                frame_idx = int(img_file.stem) # es. '000001' -> 1

                if split_name == 'train':
                    # Esempio: tiene frame 1, 11, 21... (se il resto della div ÃƒÂ¨ 1)
                    # Oppure frame 10, 20, 30... (se resto ÃƒÂ¨ 0). 
                    # Usiamo (frame_idx - 1) % RATE == 0 per prendere il primo frame sicuro.
                    if (frame_idx - 1) % TRAIN_SUBSAMPLE_RATE != 0:
                        continue 
                
                # Nome univoco: SequenceName_ImageName.jpg
                new_filename = f"{seq_name}_{img_file.name}"
                
                # Copia immagine
                shutil.copy(img_file, dest_img_dir / new_filename)
                
                # Crea file label (.txt)
                label_filename = f"{seq_name}_{img_file.stem}.txt"
                label_path = dest_lbl_dir / label_filename
                
                content = frames_data.get(frame_idx, [])
                
                with open(label_path, 'w') as lf:
                    if content:
                        lf.write("\n".join(content))
                    else:
                        # File vuoto = nessuna detection in questo frame (Background image)
                        # YOLO lo usa per capire dove NON c'ÃƒÂ¨ nulla
                        pass 

    # 4. Eseguiamo la conversione per entrambi gli split
    process_split(train_seqs, 'train')
    process_split(val_seqs, 'val')

    # 5. Creazione dataset.yaml per Ultralytics
    # Usiamo path assoluti o relativi al file yaml
    yaml_content = f"""
path: {YOLO_DATASET_PATH.as_posix()}
train: images/train
val: images/val
names:
  0: player
"""
    yaml_path = YOLO_DATASET_PATH / "dataset.yaml"
    with open(yaml_path, 'w') as f:
        f.write(yaml_content)

    print("------------------------------------------------")
    print("Conversione Completata!")
    print(f"Dataset YOLO pronto in: {YOLO_DATASET_PATH}")
    print(f"File configurazione: {yaml_path}")
    print("------------------------------------------------")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    convert_dataset_from_manifest()


================================================
FILE: src/Dataset/create_smart_splits.py
================================================
# =================================================
# FILE: src/Dataset/create_smart_splits.py
# =================================================
import os
import random
import sys
import numpy as np
import torch
import torch.nn as nn
from torchvision import models, transforms
from PIL import Image
from pathlib import Path
from sklearn.cluster import KMeans
from sklearn.metrics import silhouette_score
from sklearn.decomposition import PCA
import matplotlib.pyplot as plt
from collections import defaultdict
from tqdm import tqdm

# Setup path
ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.resolve()
sys.path.append(str(ROOT_DIR))
from config import DATASET_TR23_TRAIN, SPLIT_DIR, OUTPUT_DIR
from src.utils.get_best_device import get_best_device

# Parametri
VAL_RATIO = 0.2
SEED = 42
FRAMES_PER_SEQ = 3  
MIN_CLUSTERS = 2
MAX_CLUSTERS = 10   

def extract_features(dataset_path):
    device = get_best_device()
    print(f"Caricamento modello ResNet18 su {device}...")
    
    model = models.resnet18(weights=models.ResNet18_Weights.IMAGENET1K_V1)
    model = nn.Sequential(*list(model.children())[:-1]) 
    model.to(device)
    model.eval()
    
    preprocess = transforms.Compose([
        transforms.Resize(256),
        transforms.CenterCrop(224),
        transforms.ToTensor(),
        transforms.Normalize(mean=[0.485, 0.456, 0.406], std=[0.229, 0.224, 0.225]),
    ])

    features_dict = {} # Mappa seq_name -> feature_vector
    
    seq_dirs = sorted([s for s in os.listdir(dataset_path) if (dataset_path / s).is_dir()])
    
    print(f"Estrazione feature da {len(seq_dirs)} sequenze...")
    
    with torch.no_grad():
        for seq in tqdm(seq_dirs):
            img_dir = dataset_path / seq / "img1"
            all_images = sorted(list(img_dir.glob("*.jpg")))
            
            if not all_images: continue
            
            indices = np.linspace(0, len(all_images) - 1, FRAMES_PER_SEQ, dtype=int)
            selected_imgs = [all_images[i] for i in indices]
            
            seq_feats = []
            for img_path in selected_imgs:
                try:
                    img = Image.open(img_path).convert('RGB')
                    img_t = preprocess(img).unsqueeze(0).to(device)
                    emb = model(img_t).flatten().cpu().numpy()
                    seq_feats.append(emb)
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if seq_feats:
                avg_feat = np.mean(seq_feats, axis=0)
                features_dict[seq] = avg_feat

    return features_dict

def find_best_k(features, min_k=2, max_k=10):
    print("\n--- Ricerca Automatica K (Clustering) ---")
    best_k = min_k
    best_score = -1
    best_labels = None
    
    limit_k = min(len(features), max_k)
    if len(features) < 2: return 1, np.zeros(len(features))

    for k in range(min_k, limit_k + 1):
        kmeans = KMeans(n_clusters=k, random_state=SEED, n_init=10)
        labels = kmeans.fit_predict(features)
        score = silhouette_score(features, labels)
        if score > best_score:
            best_score = score
            best_k = k
            best_labels = labels
            
    print(f"--> Miglior K trovato: {best_k} (Silhouette Score: {best_score:.4f})")
    return best_k, best_labels

def visualize_split_quality(features_dict, train_seqs, val_seqs):
    """
    Genera un grafico 2D per mostrare quanto Train e Val si sovrappongono.
    """
    print("\nGenerazione grafico di qualitÃƒÂ  dello split...")
    
    all_seqs = train_seqs + val_seqs
    X = np.array([features_dict[s] for s in all_seqs])
    
    # Riduciamo a 2 dimensioni con PCA per poter disegnare
    pca = PCA(n_components=2)
    X_pca = pca.fit_transform(X)
    
    # Creiamo due liste di coordinate
    train_indices = [i for i, s in enumerate(all_seqs) if s in train_seqs]
    val_indices = [i for i, s in enumerate(all_seqs) if s in val_seqs]
    
    plt.figure(figsize=(10, 8))
    
    # Disegna Train (Blu)
    plt.scatter(X_pca[train_indices, 0], X_pca[train_indices, 1], 
                c='blue', alpha=0.5, label='Train Set', s=50)
    
    # Disegna Val (Rosso)
    plt.scatter(X_pca[val_indices, 0], X_pca[val_indices, 1], 
                c='red', marker='x', alpha=0.9, label='Val Set', s=70)
    
    plt.title(f"Distribuzione Train vs Val (PCA)\nObiettivo: I punti rossi devono essere ben distribuiti tra i blu")
    plt.xlabel("Principal Component 1")
    plt.ylabel("Principal Component 2")
    plt.legend()
    plt.grid(True, alpha=0.3)
    
    output_plot = OUTPUT_DIR / "split_quality_check.png"
    if not OUTPUT_DIR.exists(): OUTPUT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True)
    
    plt.savefig(output_plot)
    print(f"--> Grafico salvato in: {output_plot}")
    print("    APRILO! Se i punti rossi coprono bene l'area dei blu, lo split ÃƒÂ¨ ottimo.")

def create_smart_splits():
    if not DATASET_TR23_TRAIN.exists():
        print(f"Dataset non trovato in {DATASET_TR23_TRAIN}")
        return

    # 1. Estrazione Features
    features_map = extract_features(DATASET_TR23_TRAIN)
    sequences = sorted(list(features_map.keys()))
    features_matrix = np.array([features_map[s] for s in sequences])
    
    if len(sequences) == 0: return

    # 2. Clustering
    n_clusters, clusters = find_best_k(features_matrix, MIN_CLUSTERS, MAX_CLUSTERS)
    
    clustered_seqs = defaultdict(list)
    for seq, cluster_id in zip(sequences, clusters):
        clustered_seqs[cluster_id].append(seq)
        
    # 3. Stratified Split
    train_final = []
    val_final = []
    
    print("\n--- Generazione Split ---")
    for cid in sorted(clustered_seqs.keys()):
        seqs_in_cluster = clustered_seqs[cid]
        random.seed(SEED)
        random.shuffle(seqs_in_cluster)
        
        n_val = int(len(seqs_in_cluster) * VAL_RATIO)
        if n_val == 0 and len(seqs_in_cluster) > 1: n_val = 1
            
        val_subset = seqs_in_cluster[:n_val]
        train_subset = seqs_in_cluster[n_val:]
        
        val_final.extend(val_subset)
        train_final.extend(train_subset)
        print(f"Cluster {cid}: {len(train_subset)} Train, {len(val_subset)} Val")

    # 4. Salva i file
    SPLIT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(SPLIT_DIR / "train.txt", "w") as f: f.write("\n".join(sorted(train_final)))
    with open(SPLIT_DIR / "val.txt", "w") as f: f.write("\n".join(sorted(val_final)))
    
    # 5. VERIFICA VISIVA (Nuovo!)
    visualize_split_quality(features_map, train_final, val_final)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    create_smart_splits()


================================================
FILE: src/Dataset/create_splits.py
================================================
# =================================================
# FILE: src/Dataset/create_splits.py
# =================================================
import os
import random
from config import DATASET_TR23_TRAIN, SPLIT_DIR

# --- PARAMETRI NUOVI (Da mettere in config.py se vuoi, o lasciare qui) ---
VAL_RATIO = 0.2  # Percentuale di dati da usare per la validazione (20%)
SEED = 42        # Numero magico per la riproducibilitÃƒÂ 
# -------------------------------------------------------------------------

def generate_manifests():
    print(f"Generazione manifest partendo da: {DATASET_TR23_TRAIN}")
    
    if not DATASET_TR23_TRAIN.exists():
        print(f"ERRORE: La cartella dataset {DATASET_TR23_TRAIN} non esiste.")
        return

    # 1. Leggi tutte le cartelle (sequenze)
    sequences = [s for s in os.listdir(DATASET_TR23_TRAIN) if (DATASET_TR23_TRAIN / s).is_dir()]
    
    # 2. Ordina la lista (FONDAMENTALE per avere coerenza tra sistemi diversi)
    sequences = sorted(sequences)
    
    # 3. Mescola in modo deterministico
    random.seed(SEED)
    random.shuffle(sequences)
    
    # 4. Calcola il punto di taglio
    split_idx = int(len(sequences) * (1 - VAL_RATIO))
    train_seqs = sequences[:split_idx]
    val_seqs = sequences[split_idx:]
    
    # 5. Prepara cartella output
    SPLIT_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    # 6. Scrivi i file
    train_file = SPLIT_DIR / "train.txt"
    val_file = SPLIT_DIR / "val.txt"
    
    with open(train_file, "w") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(train_seqs))
        
    with open(val_file, "w") as f:
        f.write("\n".join(val_seqs))
        
    print("------------------------------------------------")
    print(f"Manifest creati con successo in: {SPLIT_DIR}")
    print(f"Train sequences ({len(train_seqs)}): salvate in train.txt")
    print(f"Val sequences   ({len(val_seqs)}): salvate in val.txt")
    print("------------------------------------------------")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    generate_manifests()

================================================
FILE: src/Detector/grounding_DINO/inference.py
================================================
import os
import sys
import shutil
import torch
import warnings
from PIL import Image
from transformers import AutoProcessor, AutoModelForZeroShotObjectDetection
from pathlib import Path

# --- SETUP PATH ---
current_file = Path(__file__).resolve()
project_root = current_file.parent.parent.parent.parent
sys.path.append(str(project_root))

warnings.filterwarnings("ignore", category=UserWarning, module="torchvision")

try:
    from config import *
except ImportError:
    print("ERRORE CRITICO: Non riesco a trovare 'config.py'.")
    sys.exit(1)

def run_grounding_dino_detection():
    # Setup del modello
    model_id = "IDEA-Research/grounding-dino-base"
    
    if torch.backends.mps.is_available():
        device = 'mps'
        print("Uso accelerazione Apple Silicon (MPS)")
    elif torch.cuda.is_available():
        device = 'cuda'
        print("Uso accelerazione CUDA")
    else:
        device = 'cpu'
        print("Uso CPU (Lento)")

    print(f"Caricamento Grounding DINO ({model_id})...")
    
    processor = AutoProcessor.from_pretrained(model_id)
    model = AutoModelForZeroShotObjectDetection.from_pretrained(model_id).to(device)

    TEXT_PROMPT = "person."  
    BOX_THRESHOLD = 0.35   
    TEXT_THRESHOLD = 0.25  

    base_path = os.path.join(DATASET_TR23_TEST, "test")
    output_folder = Path(DETECTOR_OUTPUT_DIR) / "grounding_dino_base"
    
    print(f"\n--- DEBUG PATH ---")
    print(f"Path Dataset: {base_path}")
    print(f"Path Output:  {output_folder}")
    
    if not os.path.exists(base_path):
        print(f"ERRORE: La cartella del dataset NON esiste: {base_path}")
        return

    sequences = os.listdir(base_path)
    print(f"Ho trovato {len(sequences)} elementi nella cartella del dataset.")

    if not os.path.exists(output_folder):
        os.makedirs(output_folder)

    files_created = 0

    for seq_name in sequences:
        seq_path = os.path.join(base_path, seq_name)
        img_source = os.path.join(seq_path, 'img1')

        if not os.path.isdir(seq_path): continue
        if not os.path.exists(img_source):
            print(f"  -> SALTATA {seq_name}: Manca cartella img1")
            continue
        
        # Filtra solo immagini vere
        image_files = sorted([f for f in os.listdir(img_source) if f.lower().endswith(('.jpg', '.jpeg', '.png'))])
        num_imgs = len(image_files)
        
        if num_imgs == 0:
            print(f"  -> SALTATA {seq_name}: Cartella vuota")
            continue

        print(f"\nAnalisi sequenza: {seq_name} ({num_imgs} immagini)")
        
        res_file_path = os.path.join(output_folder, f"{seq_name}.txt")
        
        with open(res_file_path, 'w') as f:
            lines_written = 0
            
            for i, img_name in enumerate(image_files):
                if i % 10 == 0: 
                    print(f"     Elaborazione frame {i}/{num_imgs}...", end='\r')

                img_path = os.path.join(img_source, img_name)
                
                try:
                    frame_id = int(img_name.split('.')[0])
                except ValueError:
                    continue 

                try:
                    image_pil = Image.open(img_path).convert("RGB")
                    
                    inputs = processor(images=image_pil, text=TEXT_PROMPT, return_tensors="pt").to(device)
                    
                    with torch.no_grad():
                        outputs = model(**inputs)

                    width, height = image_pil.size
                    
                    results = processor.post_process_grounded_object_detection(
                        outputs,
                        inputs.input_ids,
                        target_sizes=[(height, width)]
                    )[0]

                    for score, label, box in zip(results["scores"], results["labels"], results["boxes"]):
                        
                        if score.item() < BOX_THRESHOLD:
                            continue
                        
                        if "person" in label:
                            x1, y1, x2, y2 = box.cpu().numpy()
                            w = x2 - x1
                            h = y2 - y1
                            conf = score.item()

                            # Scrittura formato MOT
                            # frame_id, id_obj(-1), x1, y1, w, h, conf, -1, -1, -1
                            line = f"{frame_id},-1,{x1:.2f},{y1:.2f},{w:.2f},{h:.2f},{conf:.4f},-1,1,-1\n"
                            f.write(line)
                            lines_written += 1
                            
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"\nErrore nel frame {img_name}: {e}")
                    continue

            print(f"     COMPLETATO! Scritte {lines_written} righe.        ")
            files_created += 1

    print(f"\n--- FINE ---")
    print(f"File salvati in: {output_folder}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_grounding_dino_detection()

# src/Detector/YOLO/train_tuned.py
from ultralytics import YOLO
from ultralytics.utils import metrics
from pathlib import Path
import sys

# Setup path
ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent.resolve()
sys.path.append(str(ROOT_DIR))
from config import *

def custom_fitness(x):
    """
    x = [P, R, mAP@50, mAP@50-95]
    Di default YOLO usa: 0.1 * mAP@50 + 0.9 * mAP@50-95
    Noi usiamo: 1.0 * mAP@50 + 0.0 * mAP@50-95
    """
    return x[2] # Restituisce solo mAP@50

# Sovrascriviamo la funzione della libreria
metrics.fitness = custom_fitness

def train_yolo_tuned():
    dataset_yaml = YOLO_DATASET_PATH / "dataset.yaml"
    
    model_name = 'yolo12m.pt' 
    model = YOLO(MODEL_PATH / model_name) 

    print(f"Avvio Training Tuned (Loss Custom) di {model_name}...")
    
    results = model.train(
        data=str(dataset_yaml),
        epochs=50,
        imgsz=1024,
        batch=8,
        
        # --- TUNING DEI PESI DELLA LOSS (Il tuo tocco magico) ---
        box=7.5,    # (Default): Allows slightly looser boxes, increases recall
        cls=1.5,    # INCREASED: Penalize missing players (FN) heavily
        dfl=1.5,    # SLIGHTLY REDUCED: We don't need pixel-perfect distribution

        # --- Augmentation to help small objects ---
        scale=0.8,  # Allow significant zooming in during training
        degrees=0.0,
            
        # --- Altri parametri di stabilitÃƒÂ  ---
        optimizer='AdamW',
        lr0=0.0005, 
        freeze=10,  # Congeliamo il backbone per stabilitÃƒÂ 
        
        project=str(MODEL_PATH / "training_runs"),
        name='soccernet_high_precision',
        exist_ok=True,
        plots=True,
        device=0,
        workers=4
    )
    print("Training completato.")
    

if __name__ == "__main__":
    train_yolo_tuned()

FILE: src/Detector/YOLO/inference.py

import os
from ultralytics import YOLO
import torch
from config import *
import shutil
import sys
from src.utils.get_best_device import get_best_device

def run_detection_and_save_txt():
    # Carica il modello
    DEVICE = get_best_device()
    model_name = 'best.pt'  # Puoi cambiare con il percorso del tuo modello
    model = YOLO(MODEL_PATH / "training_runs" / "soccernet_high_precision" / "weights" / model_name) 
    model.to(DEVICE)
    print(f"Modello caricato su: {model.device}")

    base_path = DATASET_TR23_TEST
    output_folder = DETECTOR_OUTPUT_DIR / model_name.split(".")[0] # Cartella dove salveremo i txt per TrackEval
    
    if not os.path.exists(output_folder):
        os.makedirs(output_folder)
    else:
        # Se la cartella esiste giÃƒÂ , controlliamo se ÃƒÂ¨ vuota
        if len(os.listdir(output_folder)) > 0:
            print(f"\nATTENZIONE: La cartella di output '{output_folder}' contiene giÃƒÂ  dei file.")
            conferma = input("Vuoi eliminare TUTTI i file presenti e continuare? (y/n): ").strip().lower()

            if conferma in ['s', 'si', 'y', 'yes']:
                print("Eliminazione dei file in corso...")
                for filename in os.listdir(output_folder):
                    file_path = os.path.join(output_folder, filename)
                    try:
                        if os.path.isfile(file_path) or os.path.islink(file_path):
                            os.unlink(file_path) # Elimina file o link simbolici
                        elif os.path.isdir(file_path):
                            shutil.rmtree(file_path) # Elimina sottocartelle
                    except Exception as e:
                        print(f"Impossibile eliminare {file_path}. Motivo: {e}")
                print("Cartella svuotata con successo.\n")
            else:
                print("Operazione annullata. Esecuzione interrotta.")
                sys.exit() # Esce dallo script


    if not os.path.exists(base_path):
        print(f"Errore: Percorso {base_path} non trovato.")
        return

    sequences = os.listdir(base_path)

    for seq_name in sequences:
        seq_path = os.path.join(base_path, seq_name)
        img_source = os.path.join(seq_path, 'img1')

        if os.path.isdir(seq_path) and os.path.exists(img_source):
            print(f"--> Elaborazione sequenza: {seq_name}")
            
            # File di output per questa sequenza
            res_file_path = os.path.join(output_folder, f"{seq_name}.txt")
            
            # Eseguiamo l'inferenza usando lo stream per non saturare la RAM
            # stream=True restituisce un generatore
            results = model.predict(source=img_source, conf=0.35, imgsz=1280, verbose=False, stream=True, device=0, classes=[0])
            
            with open(res_file_path, 'w') as f:
                for result in results:
                    # Otteniamo il numero del frame dal percorso del file
                    # Es: '.../000001.jpg' -> frame_id = 1
                    path = result.path
                    frame_id = int(os.path.basename(path).split('.')[0])
                    
                    # I box sono in formato xywh (x_center, y_center, width, height) o xyxy
                    # Per MOT serve: Top-Left X, Top-Left Y, Width, Height
                    boxes = result.boxes.cpu().numpy()
                    
                    for box in boxes:
                        # Estrai coordinate xywh (top-left)
                        # box.xywh restituisce centro; box.xyxy restituisce angoli.
                        # box.xywhn normalizzato. 
                        # Ultralytics ha xywh come (center_x, center_y, w, h).
                        # Noi vogliamo (top_left_x, top_left_y, w, h).
                        
                        x1, y1, x2, y2 = box.xyxy[0]
                        w = x2 - x1
                        h = y2 - y1
                        conf = box.conf[0]
                        cls = box.cls[0]

                        # FILTRO: Assicuriamoci di salvare solo la classe "persona" (id 0 in COCO)
                        if int(cls) == 0: 
                            # Scriviamo nel formato MOT:
                            # frame, id, x, y, w, h, conf, -1, -1, -1
                            # Dato che ÃƒÂ¨ detection pura, ID mettiamo -1 o 1, non importa per DetA
                            line = f"{frame_id},-1,{x1:.2f},{y1:.2f},{w:.2f},{h:.2f},{conf:.4f},-1,1,-1\n"
                            f.write(line)
            
            print(f"Salvati risultati in: {res_file_path}")

            with open(SEQMAP_FILE, 'a') as seqmap_file:
                seqmap_file.write(f"{seq_name}\n")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_detection_and_save_txt()

# src/Detector/YOLO/train.py
from ultralytics import YOLO
from pathlib import Path
import sys

# Aggiungi root al path per importare config
ROOT_DIR = Path(__file__).parent.parent.parent.parent.resolve()
sys.path.append(str(ROOT_DIR))
from config import *

def train_yolo():
    # Percorso del dataset convertito (generato dallo script precedente)
    dataset_yaml = YOLO_DATASET_PATH / "dataset.yaml"
    
    if not dataset_yaml.exists():
        print("Errore: Dataset YAML non trovato. Esegui prima convert_to_yolo.py")
        return

    # Carica modello pre-addestrato (es. yolov8m.pt o quello che usi)
    # Nota: Assicurati che il nome del modello sia corretto (yolo12m non esiste ufficialmente, forse intendi v8 o v11?)
    model_name = 'yolo12m.pt' 
    model = YOLO(MODEL_PATH / model_name) 

    print(f"Avvio training di {model_name}...")
    
    # Parametri training
    results = model.train(
        data=str(dataset_yaml),
        epochs=50,             # Regola in base al tempo (50-100 ÃƒÂ¨ buono)
        imgsz=640,             # O 1280 se hai GPU potente e vuoi vedere i piccoli dettagli
        batch=8,              # Regola in base alla VRAM
        project=str(MODEL_PATH / "training_runs"),
        name='soccernet_finetune',
        exist_ok=True,
        device=0,              # GPU id
        plots=True,
        patience=10,            # Early stopping se non migliora per 10 epoche
        cache=False,
        workers=4
    )
    
    print("Training completato.")
    # Il modello migliore sarÃƒÂ  salvato in models/training_runs/soccernet_finetune/weights/best.pt

if __name__ == "__main__":
    train_yolo()

import sys
import os
import numpy as np
from trackeval.datasets import MotChallenge2DBox
from trackeval.metrics import HOTA
import trackeval
from config import *

def run_evaluation():
    # --- CONFIGURAZIONE PATH ---
    # GT_FOLDER deve puntare alla cartella che CONTIENE le sequenze (SNMOT-XXX)
    gt_folder_abs = DATASET_TR23_TEST 
    trackers_folder_abs = TRACKER_OUTPUT_DIR 
    seqmap_folder_abs = SEQMAP_DIR 
    
    print(f"Cercando GT in: {gt_folder_abs}") 
    print(f"Cercando Seqmap in: {seqmap_folder_abs}")

    # Verifica veloce
    if not os.path.exists(os.path.join(gt_folder_abs, 'SNMOT-116', 'seqinfo.ini')):
        print("ATTENZIONE: seqinfo.ini non trovato nemmeno nel path assoluto per 116!")

    eval_config = {
        'USE_PARALLEL': False,
        'NUM_PARALLEL_CORES': 1,
        'PRINT_RESULTS': False, # Mettiamo False per stampare solo i nostri dati puliti
        'PRINT_ONLY_COMBINED': False,
        'OUTPUT_SUMMARY': True,
        'OUTPUT_EMPTY_CLASSES': False,
        'OUTPUT_DETAILED': False,
        'PLOT_CURVES': False,
    }

    dataset_config = {
        'GT_FOLDER': gt_folder_abs, 
        'TRACKERS_FOLDER': trackers_folder_abs,
        'OUTPUT_FOLDER': None,
        'TRACKERS_TO_EVAL': None, 
        'CLASSES_TO_EVAL': ['pedestrian'], 
        'BENCHMARK': 'MOT17', 
        'SPLIT_TO_EVAL': 'test',
        'INPUT_AS_ZIP': False,
        'PRINT_CONFIG': False,
        'DO_PREPROC': False,
        'TRACKER_SUB_FOLDER': '', 
        'OUTPUT_SUB_FOLDER': '',
        'OUTPUT_SUMMARY': True, 
        'SKIP_SPLIT_FOL': True, 
        'SEQ_INFO': None,
        'SUB_FOLDER': 'gt', 
        'SEQMAP_FOLDER': seqmap_folder_abs, 
        'SEQMAP_FILE': SEQMAP_FILE, 
    }
    
    # NOTA: Qui NON chiamiamo piÃƒÂ¹ fix_ids_for_detection_only()
    # perchÃƒÂ© per il tracking servono gli ID originali del tuo algoritmo!
    
    dataset = MotChallenge2DBox(dataset_config)
    hota = HOTA()
    
    evaluator = trackeval.Evaluator(eval_config)
    try:
        np.float = float
        np.int = int
    except AttributeError:
        pass
        
    results = evaluator.evaluate([dataset], [hota])
    
    if results:
        dataset_res = results[0]
        dataset_name = list(dataset_res.keys())[0]
        tracker_res = dataset_res[dataset_name]
        tracker_name = list(tracker_res.keys())[0]
        sequences_res = tracker_res[tracker_name]
        
        seq_name = 'COMBINED_SEQ'
        if seq_name not in sequences_res:
            seq_name = list(sequences_res.keys())[0]
            print(f"Attenzione: 'COMBINED_SEQ' non trovato. Mostro i risultati per: {seq_name}")
        
        # Percorso: Dataset -> Tracker -> Sequence -> Class -> Metric
        stats = sequences_res[seq_name]['pedestrian']['HOTA']
        
        # --- CORREZIONE CRUCIALE: INDICE 9 ---
        # TrackEval calcola 19 soglie (0.05 ... 0.95).
        # index 0 = 0.05
        # index 9 = 0.50 (Quello che vuole la competizione)
        idx = 9

        deta_score = stats['DetA'][idx] * 100 
        assa_score = stats['AssA'][idx] * 100
        hota_score = stats['HOTA'][idx] * 100

        print(f"\nMetriche Tracking @ IoU 0.5 ({tracker_name} - {seq_name}):")
        print(f"HOTA: {hota_score:.2f}%  <-- QUESTO ÃƒÂ¨ il valore per la classifica")
        print(f"DetA: {deta_score:.2f}%")
        print(f"AssA: {assa_score:.2f}%")
        
        det_re = stats['DetRe'][idx] * 100
        det_pr = stats['DetPr'][idx] * 100
        print(f"DetRe (Recall): {det_re:.2f}%")
        print(f"DetPr (Precision): {det_pr:.2f}%")

        for metric in stats.keys():
            value = stats[metric]
            
            # Controlla se il valore ÃƒÂ¨ indicizzabile (un array/lista)
            if hasattr(value, "__getitem__") and not isinstance(value, (str, bytes)):
                # Se idx=0 corrisponde a IoU 0.5, usa 0. 
                # Se sei sicuro che sia l'indice 9, tieni 9.
                try:
                    print(f"{metric}: {value[0]}") 
                except IndexError:
                    print(f"{metric}: {value}") # Se l'array ÃƒÂ¨ piÃƒÂ¹ corto di quanto previsto
            else:
                # Se ÃƒÂ¨ uno scalare (come HOTA(0)), lo stampa direttamente
                print(f"{metric}: {value}")
            
        

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_evaluation()


================================================
FILE: src/Eval/run_eval_detection_only.py
================================================
import sys
import os
import numpy as np
from trackeval.datasets import MotChallenge2DBox
from trackeval.metrics import HOTA
import trackeval
from config import * # Assicurati che config.py sia accessibile

def fix_ids_for_detection_only(trackers_folder):
    """
    Scansiona i file .txt dei risultati e assegna un ID univoco a ogni riga.
    NOTA: Questo distrugge le informazioni di tracking temporale (AssA sarÃƒÂ  ~0),
    ma ÃƒÂ¨ perfetto se ti interessa calcolare SOLO la DetA (Detection Accuracy).
    """
    import glob
    
    txt_files = glob.glob(os.path.join(trackers_folder, '**', '*.txt'), recursive=True)
    
    if not txt_files:
        print(f"Nessun file .txt trovato in {trackers_folder} da correggere.")
        return

    print(f"Correzione ID per {len(txt_files)} file (Detection only mode)...")
    
    for txt_file in txt_files:
        with open(txt_file, 'r') as f:
            lines = f.readlines()
        
        new_lines = []
        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
            parts = line.strip().split(',')
            if len(parts) < 6: continue
            
            # Assegna un ID progressivo unico per riga (rompe il tracking, ok per detection)
            parts[1] = str(i + 1) 
            
            new_lines.append(','.join(parts) + '\n')
            
        with open(txt_file, 'w') as f:
            f.writelines(new_lines)
    
    print("Correzione ID completata.")

def run_evaluation():
    # --- CONFIGURAZIONE PATH ---
    gt_folder_abs = DATASET_TR23_TEST 
    trackers_folder_abs = DETECTOR_OUTPUT_DIR 
    seqmap_folder_abs = SEQMAP_DIR 
    
    print(f"GT Folder: {gt_folder_abs}") 
    print(f"Seqmap Folder: {seqmap_folder_abs}")

    # Configurazione di base per l'evaluator
    eval_config = {
        'USE_PARALLEL': False,
        'NUM_PARALLEL_CORES': 1,
        'PRINT_RESULTS': False,  # Mettiamo False per non intasare la console, stampiamo noi i dati custom
        'PRINT_ONLY_COMBINED': False,
        'OUTPUT_SUMMARY': True,
        'OUTPUT_EMPTY_CLASSES': False,
        'OUTPUT_DETAILED': False,
        'PLOT_CURVES': True,
    }

    dataset_config = {
        'GT_FOLDER': gt_folder_abs, 
        'TRACKERS_FOLDER': trackers_folder_abs,
        'OUTPUT_FOLDER': None,
        'TRACKERS_TO_EVAL': None, 
        'CLASSES_TO_EVAL': ['pedestrian'], 
        'BENCHMARK': 'MOT17', 
        'SPLIT_TO_EVAL': 'test',
        'INPUT_AS_ZIP': False,
        'PRINT_CONFIG': False,
        'DO_PREPROC': False,
        'TRACKER_SUB_FOLDER': '', 
        'OUTPUT_SUB_FOLDER': '',
        'OUTPUT_SUMMARY': True, 
        'SKIP_SPLIT_FOL': True, 
        'SEQ_INFO': None,
        'SUB_FOLDER': 'gt', 
        'SEQMAP_FOLDER': seqmap_folder_abs, 
        'SEQMAP_FILE': SEQMAP_FILE, 
    }

    # Eseguiamo la correzione degli ID per calcolare la Detection pura
    fix_ids_for_detection_only(trackers_folder_abs)
    
    dataset = MotChallenge2DBox(dataset_config)
    hota = HOTA()
    
    evaluator = trackeval.Evaluator(eval_config)
    
    # Fix per compatibilitÃƒÂ  numpy
    try:
        np.float = float
        np.int = int
    except AttributeError:
        pass

    # Esecuzione
    results = evaluator.evaluate([dataset], [hota])
    
    if results:
        dataset_res = results[0]
        dataset_name = list(dataset_res.keys())[0]
        tracker_res = dataset_res[dataset_name]
        tracker_name = list(tracker_res.keys())[0]
        sequences_res = tracker_res[tracker_name]
        
        seq_name = 'COMBINED_SEQ'
        if seq_name not in sequences_res:
            seq_name = list(sequences_res.keys())[0]
            print(f"Attenzione: COMBINED_SEQ non trovato. Uso: {seq_name}")
        
        # Estrazione metriche HOTA
        stats = sequences_res[seq_name]['pedestrian']['HOTA']
        
        # --- MODIFICA CRUCIALE PER ESTRARRE SOLO 0.5 ---
        # L'array 'DetA' ha 19 valori corrispondenti alle soglie:
        # 0.05, 0.10, ..., 0.50, ..., 0.95
        # L'indice 9 corrisponde esattamente alla soglia 0.50
        index_05 = 9 
        
        # Estrazione valori specifici all'indice 9
        deta_05 = stats['DetA'][index_05] * 100
        det_re_05 = stats['DetRe'][index_05] * 100
        det_pr_05 = stats['DetPr'][index_05] * 100
        
        # NOTA: Calcoliamo anche HOTA e AssA a 0.5, ma attenzione:
        # PoichÃƒÂ© usi "fix_ids_for_detection_only", AssA sarÃƒÂ  bassissima (~0),
        # e quindi anche HOTA sarÃƒÂ  bassissima. 
        # Tuttavia, DetA sarÃƒÂ  corretta.
        hota_05 = stats['HOTA'][index_05] * 100
        assa_05 = stats['AssA'][index_05] * 100

        print("-" * 40)
        print(f"RISULTATI SPECIFICI PER SOGLIA IoU = 0.5 ({tracker_name})")
        print("-" * 40)
        print(f"DetA @ 0.5 (Detection Accuracy):  {deta_05:.2f}%")
        print(f"DetRe @ 0.5 (Recall):             {det_re_05:.2f}%")
        print(f"DetPr @ 0.5 (Precision):          {det_pr_05:.2f}%")
        print("-" * 40)
        print("Metriche di Tracking (saranno basse se hai resettato gli ID):")
        print(f"AssA @ 0.5 (Association Acc):     {assa_05:.2f}%")
        print(f"HOTA @ 0.5:                       {hota_05:.2f}%")
        print("-" * 40)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_evaluation()


================================================
FILE: src/Tracker/visualize_result.py
================================================
import cv2
import os
import sys
import numpy as np
from pathlib import Path
from tqdm import tqdm

# --- SETUP PATH per importare config ---
current_file = Path(__file__).resolve()
project_root = current_file.parent.parent.parent
sys.path.append(str(project_root))

from config import *

def visualize_tracking():
    # --- CONFIGURAZIONE VISIVA ---
    # BGR format (Blue, Green, Red)
    # Magenta/Fucsia ÃƒÂ¨ ottimo contro il verde
    BOX_COLOR = (255, 0, 255) 
    TEXT_COLOR = (255, 0, 255)
    THICKNESS = 2
    FONT_SCALE = 0.6
    
    # --- CONFIGURAZIONE CARTELLE ---
    
    # Cerchiamo la cartella corretta in TRACKER_OUTPUT_DIR
    # Se non sei sicuro del nome, questo snippet prende l'ultima modificata
    all_subdirs = [d for d in TRACKER_OUTPUT_DIR.iterdir() if d.is_dir()]
    if not all_subdirs:
        print("Nessuna cartella risultati trovata in output.")
        return
    
    # Ordina per ultima modifica (cosÃƒÂ¬ prende l'ultimo run fatto)
    latest_subdir = max(all_subdirs, key=os.path.getmtime)
    print(f"UserÃƒÂ² i risultati dalla cartella: {latest_subdir.name}")
    
    trackers_folder = latest_subdir
    
    # 2. Cartella dove salvare i video
    video_output_dir = OUTPUT_DIR / "visualization_videos"
    video_output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)

    # 3. Cartella delle immagini originali
    base_data_path = DATASET_TR23_TEST

    # Ottieni lista sequenze dai risultati txt
    txt_files = sorted(list(trackers_folder.glob("*.txt")))
    
    if not txt_files:
        print(f"Nessun file .txt trovato in {trackers_folder}")
        return

    print(f"Trovate {len(txt_files)} sequenze da visualizzare.")

    for txt_file in txt_files:
        seq_name = txt_file.stem # es. "SNMOT-060"
        print(f"\n--> Generazione video per: {seq_name}")
        
        # Percorso immagini originali
        img_dir = base_data_path / seq_name / "img1"
        if not img_dir.exists():
            print(f"ATTENZIONE: Immagini non trovate per {seq_name}. Salto.")
            continue
            
        # Carica le detection/tracking dal txt
        # Formato MOT: frame, id, x, y, w, h, ...
        # Creiamo un dizionario: frame_id -> lista di [id, x, y, w, h]
        tracks = {}
        with open(txt_file, 'r') as f:
            for line in f:
                parts = line.strip().split(',')
                if len(parts) < 6: continue
                
                frame_idx = int(parts[0])
                obj_id = int(parts[1])
                x = float(parts[2])
                y = float(parts[3])
                w = float(parts[4])
                h = float(parts[5])
                
                if frame_idx not in tracks:
                    tracks[frame_idx] = []
                tracks[frame_idx].append((obj_id, x, y, w, h))

        # Lista immagini
        img_files = sorted([f for f in img_dir.glob("*.jpg")])
        if not img_files:
            continue

        # Setup Video Writer
        # Leggiamo la prima immagine per le dimensioni
        first_img = cv2.imread(str(img_files[0]))
        height, width, _ = first_img.shape
        
        save_path = video_output_dir / f"{seq_name}.mp4"
        fourcc = cv2.VideoWriter_fourcc(*'mp4v') # Codec mp4
        out = cv2.VideoWriter(str(save_path), fourcc, 25.0, (width, height)) # 25 FPS standard

        for img_path in tqdm(img_files, desc="Rendering frames"):
            frame_id = int(img_path.stem) # es 00001 -> 1
            
            frame = cv2.imread(str(img_path))
            
            # Se ci sono detection per questo frame, disegnale
            if frame_id in tracks:
                for (obj_id, x, y, w, h) in tracks[frame_id]:
                    # Coordinate intere per OpenCV
                    x1 = int(x)
                    y1 = int(y)
                    x2 = int(x + w)
                    y2 = int(y + h)
                    
                    # 1. Disegna Box Sottile
                    cv2.rectangle(frame, (x1, y1), (x2, y2), BOX_COLOR, THICKNESS)
                    
                    # 2. Testo ID (Sopra il box)
                    label = f"{obj_id}"
                    
                    # Calcola dimensione testo per posizionarlo bene
                    (text_w, text_h), baseline = cv2.getTextSize(label, cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, FONT_SCALE, 1)
                    
                    # Posizione testo: appena sopra il box
                    text_x = x1
                    text_y = max(y1 - 5, text_h + 5) # Evita di uscire dall'immagine in alto
                    
                    # Disegna solo il testo (senza sfondo per pulizia, come richiesto)
                    # Usiamo lo stesso colore del box per coerenza
                    cv2.putText(frame, label, (text_x, text_y), 
                                cv2.FONT_HERSHEY_SIMPLEX, FONT_SCALE, TEXT_COLOR, THICKNESS)

            out.write(frame)

        out.release()
        print(f"Video salvato in: {save_path}")

    print("\nVisualizzazione completata!")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    visualize_tracking()


================================================
FILE: src/Tracker/Yolo12m/inference.py
================================================
import os
import sys
from ultralytics import YOLO
from pathlib import Path

# Setup path
current_file = Path(__file__).resolve()
project_root = current_file.parent.parent.parent.parent
sys.path.append(str(project_root))

from config import *
from src.utils.get_best_device import get_best_device

def run_optimized_tracking():
    # --- CONFIGURAZIONE ---
    # Usa il path assoluto o relativo corretto al file yaml appena creato
    CONF_THRES = 0.35
    MAX_ENTITIES = 28  # Il filtro "closed set" che abbiamo discusso
    # ----------------------

    DEVICE = get_best_device()
    model_name = 'best.pt'
    weights_path = MODEL_PATH / "training_runs" / "soccernet_high_precision" / "weights" / model_name
    
    print(f"Caricamento modello: {weights_path}")
    print(f"Configurazione Tracker Custom: {TRACKER_CONFIG}")
    
    model = YOLO(weights_path)
    model.to(DEVICE)

    output_folder = TRACKER_OUTPUT_DIR / f"{model_name.split('.')[0]}_optimized_tracking"
    if not os.path.exists(output_folder):
        os.makedirs(output_folder)

    base_path = DATASET_TR23_TEST
    sequences = sorted(os.listdir(base_path))

    for seq_name in sequences:
        seq_path = os.path.join(base_path, seq_name)
        img_source = os.path.join(seq_path, 'img1')

        if os.path.isdir(seq_path) and os.path.exists(img_source):
            print(f"--> Tracking sequenza: {seq_name}")
            res_file_path = os.path.join(output_folder, f"{seq_name}.txt")
            
            # Reset del modello per pulire la memoria della GPU/Tracker tra sequenze
            model = YOLO(weights_path)
            model.to(DEVICE)

            results = model.track(
                source=img_source,
                conf=CONF_THRES,
                iou=0.6,
                imgsz=1280,
                device=0,
                classes=[0],
                tracker=str(TRACKER_CONFIG / "soccer_tracker.yaml"), # <--- QUI CARICHIAMO LA TUA CONFIGURAZIONE
                persist=True,
                verbose=False,
                stream=True
            )
            
            with open(res_file_path, 'w') as f:
                for result in results:
                    if result.boxes is None: continue
                    
                    frame_id = int(os.path.basename(result.path).split('.')[0])
                    boxes = result.boxes.cpu().numpy()
                    
                    # --- Raccolta e Filtro Top-K (Closed Set Real-time) ---
                    valid_candidates = []
                    
                    for box in boxes:
                        # Se il box non ha ID (perso dal tracker), lo scartiamo o mettiamo -1
                        if box.id is None: continue
                        
                        track_id = int(box.id[0])
                        x1, y1, x2, y2 = box.xyxy[0]
                        w = x2 - x1
                        h = y2 - y1
                        conf = box.conf[0]
                        
                        # Filtro geometrico (cartelloni bassi e larghi)
                        if (w / h) > 1.3: continue
                        
                        valid_candidates.append({
                            'line': f"{frame_id},{track_id},{x1:.2f},{y1:.2f},{w:.2f},{h:.2f},{conf:.4f},-1,1,-1\n",
                            'conf': conf
                        })
                    
                    # Ordina per confidenza e tieni solo i top 24 (22 giocatori + arbitri)
                    valid_candidates.sort(key=lambda x: x['conf'], reverse=True)
                    final_candidates = valid_candidates[:MAX_ENTITIES]
                    
                    for cand in final_candidates:
                        f.write(cand['line'])

            print(f"Salvati: {res_file_path}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_optimized_tracking()

tracker_type: bytetrack  # Usiamo ByteTrack

new_track_thresh: 0.6    # Confidenza alta per inizializzare nuove track

import torch

def get_best_device():
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        return 'cuda'
    
    if torch.backends.mps.is_available():
        return 'mps'
    
    return 'cpu'

| <img src="https://github.com/sportsreid/sportsreid/blob/main/images/hierarchical_8921.jpg" width="450" height=240>  | | | <img src="https://github.com/sportsreid/sportsreid/blob/main/images/hierarchical_3297.jpg" width="450" height=240>  |

# Sportsreid

Sportsreid is useful for re-identifying the same player in different frames of a broadcast video of a match. This repo is built on top of [SoccerNet Re-Identification](https://github.com/SoccerNet/sn-reid) and the popular [Torchreid](https://github.com/KaiyangZhou/deep-person-reid). It is currently ranked #2 on the test split leaderboard for the SoccerNet 2022 ReIdentification challenge.

**Note to reviewers: At the time of submission of our manuscript, our approach was #1 on the test split leaderboard. Also note that our models are pretrained on only Imagenet. We do not have any such details about the approach that is currently #1 on the leaderboard.**

## Hierarchical Sampling

The hierarchical sampler is defined in [torchreid/data/sampler.py](torchreid/data/sampler.py) as [RandomIdentitySampler_Hierarchical](https://github.com/sportsreid/sportsreid/blob/dcd1fccf0d3b2921edfd089d010e9512ff61fe51/torchreid/data/sampler.py#L15). To use it, just set "train_sampler" to "RandomIdentitySampler_Hierarchical" in the yaml config file.

## Centroid loss

The centroid loss is defined in [torchreid/losses/hard_mine_triplet_loss.py](https://github.com/sportsreid/sportsreid/blob/dcd1fccf0d3b2921edfd089d010e9512ff61fe51/torchreid/losses/hard_mine_triplet_loss.py#L91). To use it, set "weight_cc" to a value > 0.0 in the yaml config file.

## Metrics, Pretrained Models and Config Files

We provide trained models and config files for different network architectures in the table below. These models were pretrained on ImageNet and then trained on the train split of the SoccerNet Re-Identification dataset.

| name | #params | Resolution | mAP | rank-1 | chkpt | config |
| ---  | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| ResNet50-fc512 | 24.6M | 256x128 | 81.8 | 76.1 | [model](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1o45E8lxB9mxJ1lfSgMpi3mC0zUwVvzgz/view?usp=sharing) | [config](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1CqtCPpn9NSlZ5NMmGUqWfd-fcOOWVyOu/view?usp=sharing) |
| OSNet_x1_0 | 2.2M | 256x128 | 83.4 | 78.0 | [model](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1To0Ww6_HxU2ITAlb4kQEgYExV-orwit8/view?usp=sharing) | [config](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1xO4Qe7f4FwpXnEe39cn24FdRDg6F-LLu/view?usp=sharing) |
| DeiT-Tiny/16 | 5.5M | 224x224 | 82.2 | 76.2 | [model](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1u6SLzk8TTZQt2NvNrE2KNOFB6S67JE1g/view?usp=sharing) | [config](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1wZCXISaGdeyQfgL1MaN7BNVKOZTBwku-/view?usp=sharing) |
| DeiT-S/16 | 21.7M | 224x224 | 84.3 | 79.4 | [model](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1yPgYoxP5a8X5p0LGdB_YHAXHDyRcShIo/view?usp=sharing) | [config](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1wZCXISaGdeyQfgL1MaN7BNVKOZTBwku-/view?usp=sharing) |
| ViT-B/16 | 57.7M | 224x224 | 86.0 | 81.5 | [model](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1yoGabayh4yRGkfzBwmBF1ocNiwwo1WVv/view?usp=sharing) | [config](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1f7JL1sBqThM9J3lL4XrTEAA2k3bVAOl3/view?usp=sharing) |
| ViT-L/16* | 303.6M | 224x224 | 89.8 | 86.7 | [model](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1NHtpTuCCueA1Q8S5li3y4L3oO9lFvte2/view?usp=sharing) | [config](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1ZdHOJwben0drx2xJcaf-UczgKzKReWY6/view?usp=sharing) |

The ViT-L/16* model is trained with 5 different random seeds for initialization and then the weights are averaged across these seeds to further increase mAP. This is inspired by this [paper](https://arxiv.org/abs/2203.05482).

## Installation

```
# cd to your preferred directory and clone this repo
git clone https://github.com/sportsreid/sportsreid.git

# create environment
cd sportsreid/
conda create --name sportsreid python=3.7
conda activate sportsreid

# install dependencies
# make sure `which python` and `which pip` point to the correct path
pip install -r requirements.txt

# install torch and torchvision (select the proper cuda version to suit your machine)
conda install pytorch torchvision cudatoolkit=9.0 -c pytorch

# install sportsreid (don't need to re-build it if you modify the source code)
python setup.py develop
```
## Training

```
python benchmarks/baseline/main.py --config-file <path to config yaml file>
```

This will automatically download the SoccerNet data as well. If you want to download data separately, please look at the SoccerNet [github page](https://github.com/SoccerNet/sn-reid)

## Inference

```
python benchmarks/baseline/main.py --config-file <path to config yaml file> test.evaluate True model.resume <path to model checkpoint>
```

FILE: sportsreid/setup.py
================================================
import numpy as np
import os.path as osp
from setuptools import setup, find_packages
from distutils.extension import Extension
from Cython.Build import cythonize


def readme():
    with open('TORCHREID_README.rst') as f:
        content = f.read()
    return content


def find_version():
    version_file = 'torchreid/__init__.py'
    with open(version_file, 'r') as f:
        exec(compile(f.read(), version_file, 'exec'))
    return locals()['__version__']


def numpy_include():
    try:
        numpy_include = np.get_include()
    except AttributeError:
        numpy_include = np.get_numpy_include()
    return numpy_include


ext_modules = [
    Extension(
        'torchreid.metrics.rank_cylib.rank_cy',
        ['torchreid/metrics/rank_cylib/rank_cy.pyx'],
        include_dirs=[numpy_include()],
    )
]


def get_requirements(filename='requirements.txt'):
    here = osp.dirname(osp.realpath(__file__))
    with open(osp.join(here, filename), 'r') as f:
        requires = [line.replace('\n', '') for line in f.readlines()]
    return requires


setup(
    name='torchreid',
    version=find_version(),
    description='A library for deep learning person re-ID in PyTorch',
    author='Kaiyang Zhou',
    license='MIT',
    long_description=readme(),
    url='https://github.com/KaiyangZhou/deep-person-reid',
    packages=find_packages(),
    install_requires=get_requirements(),
    keywords=['Person Re-Identification', 'Deep Learning', 'Computer Vision'],
    ext_modules=cythonize(ext_modules)
)

FILE: sportsreid/benchmarks/baseline/default_config.py

from yacs.config import CfgNode as CN


def get_default_config():
    cfg = CN()

    # model
    cfg.model = CN()
    cfg.model.name = 'resnet50'
    cfg.model.pretrained = True # automatically load pretrained model weights if available
    cfg.model.load_weights = '' # path to model weights
    cfg.model.resume = '' # path to checkpoint for resume training

    # data
    cfg.data = CN()
    cfg.data.type = 'image'
    cfg.data.root = 'reid-data'
    cfg.data.sources = ['soccernetv3']
    cfg.data.targets = ['soccernetv3']  # choose from ['soccernetv3', 'soccernetv3_test', 'soccernetv3_challenge']
    cfg.data.eval_metric = 'soccernetv3'  # metric for evaluation, choose from 'default', 'cuhk03', 'soccernetv3'
    cfg.data.workers = 4 # number of data loading workers
    cfg.data.split_id = 0 # split index
    cfg.data.height = 256 # image height
    cfg.data.width = 128 # image width
    cfg.data.combineall = False # combine train, query and gallery for training
    cfg.data.transforms = ['random_flip'] # data augmentation
    cfg.data.k_tfm = 1 # number of times to apply augmentation to an image independently
    cfg.data.norm_mean = [0.485, 0.456, 0.406] # default is imagenet mean
    cfg.data.norm_std = [0.229, 0.224, 0.225] # default is imagenet std
    cfg.data.save_dir = 'log' # path to save log
    cfg.data.load_train_targets = False # load training set from target dataset

    # specific datasets
    cfg.market1501 = CN()
    cfg.market1501.use_500k_distractors = False # add 500k distractors to the gallery set for market1501
    cfg.cuhk03 = CN()
    cfg.cuhk03.labeled_images = False # use labeled images, if False, use detected images
    cfg.cuhk03.classic_split = False # use classic split by Li et al. CVPR14
    cfg.soccernetv3 = CN()
    cfg.soccernetv3.training_subset = 1.0 # Use 'training_subset'% of total number of training set actions at training
    # stage. Use this option for faster training. Set to 1.0 to use full training set.

    # sampler
    cfg.sampler = CN()
    cfg.sampler.train_sampler = 'RandomIdentitySampler' # sampler for source train loader
    cfg.sampler.train_sampler_t = 'RandomIdentitySampler' # sampler for target train loader
    cfg.sampler.num_instances = 4 # number of instances per identity for RandomIdentitySampler
    cfg.sampler.num_cams = 1 # number of cameras to sample in a batch (for RandomDomainSampler)
    cfg.sampler.num_datasets = 1 # number of datasets to sample in a batch (for RandomDatasetSampler)

    # video reid setting
    cfg.video = CN()
    cfg.video.seq_len = 15 # number of images to sample in a tracklet
    cfg.video.sample_method = 'evenly' # how to sample images from a tracklet
    cfg.video.pooling_method = 'avg' # how to pool features over a tracklet

    # train
    cfg.train = CN()
    cfg.train.optim = 'adam'
    cfg.train.lr = 0.0003
    cfg.train.weight_decay = 5e-4
    cfg.train.max_epoch = 60
    cfg.train.start_epoch = 0
    cfg.train.batch_size = 32
    cfg.train.fixbase_epoch = 0 # number of epochs to fix base layers
    cfg.train.open_layers = [
        'classifier'
    ] # layers for training while keeping others frozen
    cfg.train.staged_lr = False # set different lr to different layers
    cfg.train.new_layers = ['classifier'] # newly added layers with default lr
    cfg.train.base_lr_mult = 0.1 # learning rate multiplier for base layers
    cfg.train.lr_scheduler = 'single_step'
    cfg.train.stepsize = [20] # stepsize to decay learning rate
    cfg.train.gamma = 0.1 # learning rate decay multiplier
    cfg.train.print_freq = 20 # print frequency
    cfg.train.seed = 1 # random seed
    cfg.train.warmup_lr = 0.0 # for training transformers
    cfg.train.warmup_steps = 0
    cfg.train.min_lr = 1e-6 # for training transformers
    cfg.train.warmup_epochs = 0 # for training transformers

    # optimizer
    cfg.sgd = CN()
    cfg.sgd.momentum = 0.9 # momentum factor for sgd and rmsprop
    cfg.sgd.dampening = 0. # dampening for momentum
    cfg.sgd.nesterov = False # Nesterov momentum
    cfg.rmsprop = CN()
    cfg.rmsprop.alpha = 0.99 # smoothing constant
    cfg.adam = CN()
    cfg.adam.beta1 = 0.9 # exponential decay rate for first moment
    cfg.adam.beta2 = 0.999 # exponential decay rate for second moment

    # loss
    cfg.loss = CN()
    cfg.loss.name = 'softmax'
    cfg.loss.softmax = CN()
    cfg.loss.softmax.label_smooth = True # use label smoothing regularizer
    cfg.loss.triplet = CN()
    cfg.loss.triplet.margin = 0.3 # distance margin
    cfg.loss.triplet.weight_t = 1. # weight to balance hard triplet loss
    cfg.loss.triplet.weight_x = 0. # weight to balance cross entropy loss
    cfg.loss.triplet.weight_tc = 0. # weight to balance centroid triplet loss
    cfg.loss.triplet.weight_cc = 0. # weight to balance loss from distance between centroids
    cfg.loss.triplet.topk = 1 #Mine Top k hard negatives
    cfg.loss.triplet.bottomk = 1 #Mine bottom hard positives

    # test
    cfg.test = CN()
    cfg.test.batch_size = 100
    cfg.test.dist_metric = 'euclidean' # distance metric, ['euclidean', 'cosine']
    cfg.test.normalize_feature = False # normalize feature vectors before computing distance
    cfg.test.ranks = [1, 5, 10, 20] # cmc ranks
    cfg.test.evaluate = False # test only
    cfg.test.eval_freq = -1 # evaluation frequency (-1 means to only test after training)
    cfg.test.start_eval = 0 # start to evaluate after a specific epoch
    cfg.test.rerank = False # use person re-ranking
    cfg.test.visrank = False # visualize ranked results (only available when cfg.test.evaluate=True)
    cfg.test.visrank_topk = 10 # top-k ranks to visualize
    cfg.test.export_ranking_results = False # export query to gallery ranking results to JSON file in 'data.save_dir' for each
                                    # target dataset. To be used for external evaluation and submission on EvalAI

    return cfg


def imagedata_kwargs(cfg):
    return {
        'root': cfg.data.root,
        'sources': cfg.data.sources,
        'targets': cfg.data.targets,
        'height': cfg.data.height,
        'width': cfg.data.width,
        'transforms': cfg.data.transforms,
        'k_tfm': cfg.data.k_tfm,
        'norm_mean': cfg.data.norm_mean,
        'norm_std': cfg.data.norm_std,
        'use_gpu': cfg.use_gpu,
        'split_id': cfg.data.split_id,
        'combineall': cfg.data.combineall,
        'load_train_targets': cfg.data.load_train_targets,
        'batch_size_train': cfg.train.batch_size,
        'batch_size_test': cfg.test.batch_size,
        'workers': cfg.data.workers,
        'num_instances': cfg.sampler.num_instances,
        'num_cams': cfg.sampler.num_cams,
        'num_datasets': cfg.sampler.num_datasets,
        'train_sampler': cfg.sampler.train_sampler,
        'train_sampler_t': cfg.sampler.train_sampler_t,
        # image dataset specific
        'cuhk03_labeled': cfg.cuhk03.labeled_images,
        'cuhk03_classic_split': cfg.cuhk03.classic_split,
        'market1501_500k': cfg.market1501.use_500k_distractors,
        'soccernetv3_training_subset': cfg.soccernetv3.training_subset,
    }


def videodata_kwargs(cfg):
    return {
        'root': cfg.data.root,
        'sources': cfg.data.sources,
        'targets': cfg.data.targets,
        'height': cfg.data.height,
        'width': cfg.data.width,
        'transforms': cfg.data.transforms,
        'norm_mean': cfg.data.norm_mean,
        'norm_std': cfg.data.norm_std,
        'use_gpu': cfg.use_gpu,
        'split_id': cfg.data.split_id,
        'combineall': cfg.data.combineall,
        'batch_size_train': cfg.train.batch_size,
        'batch_size_test': cfg.test.batch_size,
        'workers': cfg.data.workers,
        'num_instances': cfg.sampler.num_instances,
        'num_cams': cfg.sampler.num_cams,
        'num_datasets': cfg.sampler.num_datasets,
        'train_sampler': cfg.sampler.train_sampler,
        # video dataset specific
        'seq_len': cfg.video.seq_len,
        'sample_method': cfg.video.sample_method
    }


def optimizer_kwargs(cfg):
    return {
        'optim': cfg.train.optim,
        'lr': cfg.train.lr,
        'weight_decay': cfg.train.weight_decay,
        'momentum': cfg.sgd.momentum,
        'sgd_dampening': cfg.sgd.dampening,
        'sgd_nesterov': cfg.sgd.nesterov,
        'rmsprop_alpha': cfg.rmsprop.alpha,
        'adam_beta1': cfg.adam.beta1,
        'adam_beta2': cfg.adam.beta2,
        'staged_lr': cfg.train.staged_lr,
        'new_layers': cfg.train.new_layers,
        'base_lr_mult': cfg.train.base_lr_mult
    }


def lr_scheduler_kwargs(cfg):
    return {
        'lr_scheduler': cfg.train.lr_scheduler,
        'stepsize': cfg.train.stepsize,
        'gamma': cfg.train.gamma,
        'max_epoch': cfg.train.max_epoch
    }


def engine_run_kwargs(cfg):
    return {
        'save_dir': cfg.data.save_dir,
        'max_epoch': cfg.train.max_epoch,
        'start_epoch': cfg.train.start_epoch,
        'fixbase_epoch': cfg.train.fixbase_epoch,
        'open_layers': cfg.train.open_layers,
        'start_eval': cfg.test.start_eval,
        'eval_freq': cfg.test.eval_freq,
        'test_only': cfg.test.evaluate,
        'print_freq': cfg.train.print_freq,
        'dist_metric': cfg.test.dist_metric,
        'normalize_feature': cfg.test.normalize_feature,
        'visrank': cfg.test.visrank,
        'visrank_topk': cfg.test.visrank_topk,
        'eval_metric': cfg.data.eval_metric,
        'ranks': cfg.test.ranks,
        'rerank': cfg.test.rerank,
        'export_ranking_results': cfg.test.export_ranking_results,
    }

FILE: sportsreid/benchmarks/baseline/main.py
================================================
import sys
import time
import os.path as osp
import argparse
import torch
import torch.nn as nn

import torchreid
from torchreid.utils import (
    Logger, check_isfile, set_random_seed, collect_env_info,
    resume_from_checkpoint, load_pretrained_weights, compute_model_complexity
)

from default_config import (
    imagedata_kwargs, optimizer_kwargs, videodata_kwargs, engine_run_kwargs,
    get_default_config, lr_scheduler_kwargs
)


def build_datamanager(cfg):
    if cfg.data.type == 'image':
        return torchreid.data.ImageDataManager(**imagedata_kwargs(cfg))
    else:
        return torchreid.data.VideoDataManager(**videodata_kwargs(cfg))


def build_engine(cfg, datamanager, model, optimizer, scheduler):
    if cfg.data.type == 'image':
        if cfg.loss.name == 'softmax':
            engine = torchreid.engine.ImageSoftmaxEngine(
                datamanager,
                model,
                optimizer=optimizer,
                scheduler=scheduler,
                use_gpu=cfg.use_gpu,
                label_smooth=cfg.loss.softmax.label_smooth
            )

        else:
            engine = torchreid.engine.ImageTripletEngine(
                datamanager,
                model,
                optimizer=optimizer,
                margin=cfg.loss.triplet.margin,
                weight_t=cfg.loss.triplet.weight_t,
                weight_x=cfg.loss.triplet.weight_x,
                weight_tc=cfg.loss.triplet.weight_tc,
                weight_cc=cfg.loss.triplet.weight_cc,
                scheduler=scheduler,
                use_gpu=cfg.use_gpu,
                label_smooth=cfg.loss.softmax.label_smooth,
                topk=cfg.loss.triplet.topk,
                bottomk=cfg.loss.triplet.bottomk,
                warmup_lr = cfg.train.warmup_lr,
                warmup_steps = cfg.train.warmup_steps,
                lr = cfg.train.lr
            )

    else:
        if cfg.loss.name == 'softmax':
            engine = torchreid.engine.VideoSoftmaxEngine(
                datamanager,
                model,
                optimizer=optimizer,
                scheduler=scheduler,
                use_gpu=cfg.use_gpu,
                label_smooth=cfg.loss.softmax.label_smooth,
                pooling_method=cfg.video.pooling_method
            )

        else:
            engine = torchreid.engine.VideoTripletEngine(
                datamanager,
                model,
                optimizer=optimizer,
                margin=cfg.loss.triplet.margin,
                weight_t=cfg.loss.triplet.weight_t,
                weight_x=cfg.loss.triplet.weight_x,
                scheduler=scheduler,
                use_gpu=cfg.use_gpu,
                label_smooth=cfg.loss.softmax.label_smooth
            )

    return engine


def reset_config(cfg, args):
    if args.root:
        cfg.data.root = args.root
    if args.sources:
        cfg.data.sources = args.sources
    if args.targets:
        cfg.data.targets = args.targets
    if args.transforms:
        cfg.data.transforms = args.transforms


def check_cfg(cfg):
    if cfg.loss.name == 'triplet' and cfg.loss.triplet.weight_x == 0:
        assert cfg.train.fixbase_epoch == 0, \
            'The output of classifier is not included in the computational graph'


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(
        formatter_class=argparse.ArgumentDefaultsHelpFormatter
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        '--config-file', type=str, default='', help='path to config file'
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        '-s',
        '--sources',
        type=str,
        nargs='+',
        help='source datasets (delimited by space)'
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        '-t',
        '--targets',
        type=str,
        nargs='+',
        help='target datasets (delimited by space)'
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        '--transforms', type=str, nargs='+', help='data augmentation'
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        '--root', type=str, default='', help='path to data root'
    )
    parser.add_argument(
        'opts',
        default=None,
        nargs=argparse.REMAINDER,
        help='Modify config options using the command-line'
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()

    cfg = get_default_config()
    cfg.use_gpu = torch.cuda.is_available()
    if args.config_file:
        cfg.merge_from_file(args.config_file)
    reset_config(cfg, args)
    cfg.merge_from_list(args.opts)
    set_random_seed(cfg.train.seed)
    check_cfg(cfg)

    log_name = 'test.log' if cfg.test.evaluate else 'train.log'
    log_name += time.strftime('-%Y-%m-%d-%H-%M-%S')
    sys.stdout = Logger(osp.join(cfg.data.save_dir, log_name))

    print('Show configuration\n{}\n'.format(cfg))
    print('Collecting env info ...')
    print('** System info **\n{}\n'.format(collect_env_info()))

    if cfg.use_gpu:
        torch.backends.cudnn.benchmark = False #For reproducibility

    datamanager = build_datamanager(cfg)

    print('Building model: {}'.format(cfg.model.name))
    model = torchreid.models.build_model(
        name=cfg.model.name,
        num_classes=datamanager.num_train_pids,
        loss=cfg.loss.name,
        pretrained=cfg.model.pretrained,
        use_gpu=cfg.use_gpu,
        img_size=(cfg.data.height, cfg.data.width),
    )
    num_params, flops = compute_model_complexity(
        model, (1, 3, cfg.data.height, cfg.data.width)
    )
    print('Model complexity: params={:,} flops={:,}'.format(num_params, flops))

    if cfg.model.load_weights and check_isfile(cfg.model.load_weights):
        load_pretrained_weights(model, cfg.model.load_weights)

    if cfg.use_gpu:
        model = nn.DataParallel(model).cuda()

    optimizer = torchreid.optim.build_optimizer(model, **optimizer_kwargs(cfg))

    scheduler = torchreid.optim.build_lr_scheduler(
        optimizer, **lr_scheduler_kwargs(cfg)
    )

    if cfg.model.resume and check_isfile(cfg.model.resume):
        cfg.train.start_epoch = resume_from_checkpoint(
            cfg.model.resume, model, optimizer=optimizer, scheduler=scheduler
        )

    print(
        'Building {}-engine for {}-reid'.format(cfg.loss.name, cfg.data.type)
    )
    engine = build_engine(cfg, datamanager, model, optimizer, scheduler)
    engine.run(**engine_run_kwargs(cfg))

if __name__ == '__main__':
    main()
